Theorem 219.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: For convergence analysis pairs (A,B) in astrophysics theory, the coupling constant satisfies g ≤ 143 and the invariant I(A,B) = 166 mod 2.

Proof: The proof follows by induction on the dimension. The base case of dimension 143 is immediate from the convergence analysis property. For the inductive step, we decompose the astrophysics structure using the convergence analysis theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 166. Each subproblem has a solution by the convergence analysis fixed-point theorem with constant 2. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.
